Keep only comma removal; do not touch other punctuation.\n        return v[0] + v[1:].replace(\",\", \"\")\n    return v\n\n\nXMLNS_RE = re.compile(r\"\\sxmlns(?::(?P<pfx>[A-Za-z_][\\w.-]*))?=\\\"(?P<uri>[^\\\"]+)\\\"\")\n\n\ndef register_namespaces_from_xml_text(xml_text: str) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Register namespaces as they appear in the original XML.\n\n    Returns a prefix->uri mapping ('' for default namespace).\n    \"\"\"\n    nsmap: dict[str, str] = {}\n    for m in XMLNS_RE.finditer(xml_text):\n        pfx = m.group(\"pfx\") or \"\"\n        uri = m.group(\"uri\")\n        # ET.register_namespace expects prefix '' for default namespace.\n        if pfx not in nsmap:\n            nsmap[pfx] = uri\n            ET.register_namespace(pfx, uri)\n    return nsmap\n\n\nPLACEHOLDER_RE = re.compile(r\"{{\\s*([^{}]+?)\\s*}}\")\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass TextRun:\n    elem: ET.Element\n    start: int\n    end: int\n\n\ndef _collect_hp_t_elems(p_elem: ET.Element, hp_uri: str) -> list[ET.Element]:\n    t_tag = f\"{{{hp_uri}}}t\"\n    # Walk in document order within paragraph.\n    return [e for e in p_elem.iter() if e.tag == t_tag]\n\n\ndef _concat_text(elems: list[ET.Element]) -> str:\n    return \"\".join([(e.text or \"\") for e in elems])\n\n\ndef _compute_text_ranges(t_elems: list[ET.Element]) -> list[TextRun]:\n    ranges: list[TextRun] = []\n    pos = 0\n    for e in t_elems:\n        s = pos\n        pos += len(e.text or \"\")\n        ranges.append(TextRun(e, s, pos))\n    return ranges\n\n\ndef _placeholder_spans_multiple_nodes(full_text: str, ranges: list[TextRun]) -> bool:\n    for m in PLACEHOLDER_RE.finditer(full_text):\n        ms, me = m.span()\n        containing = [r for r in ranges if r.start <= ms and me <= r.end]\n        if not containing:\n            # Remove remaining <hp:t> elements. Must remove from their direct parent.\n    for t in t_elems[1:]:\n        parent = _find_parent(p_elem, t)\n        if parent is not None:\n            parent.remove(t)\n\n    # Also remove any now-empty <hp:run> elements (optional but keeps XML tidy).\n    run_tag = f\"{{{hp_uri}}}run\"\n    for run in list(p_elem.iter(run_tag)):\n        if len(list(run)) == 0 and (run.text is None or run.text.strip() == \"\"):\n            parent = _find_parent(p_elem, run)\n            if parent is not None:\n                parent.remove(run)\n\n    # Ensure only one <hp:t> remains with the combined text (common expectation).\n    # If the first <hp:t> was removed due to an unexpected structure, re-add it.\n    # (Should not happen in our templates, but defensive.)\n    if not any(e.tag == t_tag for e in p_elem.iter()):\n        run = ET.SubElement(p_elem, run_tag)\n        t = ET.SubElement(run, t_tag)\n        t.text = full_text\n\n\ndef _find_parent(root: ET.Element, child: ET.Element) -> ET.Element | None:\n    for p in root.iter():\n        for c in list(p):\n            if c is child:\n                return p\n    return None\n\n\ndef _replace_placeholders_in_text(text: str, data: dict) -> tuple[str, bool]:\n    changed = False\n\n    def repl(m: re.Match) -> str:\n        nonlocal changed\n        key = m.group(1)\n        if key in data:\n            val = sanitize_value(data[key])\n            changed = True\n            return str(val)\n        return m.group(0)\n\n    new_text = PLACEHOLDER_RE.sub(repl, text)\n    return new_text, changed\n\n\nDATE_RE = re.compile(r\"(?P<y>\\d{4})[.-](?P<m>\\d{1,2})\")\n\n\ndef _month_span_inclusive(start_y: int, start_m: int, end_y: int, end_m: int) -> int:\n    return (end_y - start_y) * 12 + (end_m - start_m) + 1\n\n\ndef _append_phase_months(text: str) -> tuple[str, bool]:\n    \"\"\"Append ' (N개월)' to phase lines that include a date or date range.\n\n    Supports strings like:\n      '1단계: ... (2026-08 ~ 2026-10)'\n      '2단계: ... (2026.10 ~ 2026.12)'\n      '3단계: ... (2027-01)'\n\n    Only appends if a suffix like '(N개월)' is not already present.\n    \"\"\"\n    if \"개월\" in text:\n        return text, False\n\n    # Must be a phase line.\n    if not (\"1단계\" in text or \"2단계\" in text or \"3단계\" in text or \"단계1\" in text or \"단계2\" in text or \"단계3\" in text):\n        return text, False\n\n    # Keep only comma removal; do not touch other punctuation.\n        return v[0] + v[1:].replace(\",\", \"\")\n    return v\n\n\nXMLNS_RE = re.compile(r\"\\sxmlns(?::(?P<pfx>[A-Za-z_][\\w.-]*))?=\\\"(?P<uri>[^\\\"]+)\\\"\")\n\n\ndef register_namespaces_from_xml_text(xml_text: str) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Register namespaces as they appear in the original XML.\n\n    Returns a prefix->uri mapping ('' for default namespace).\n    \"\"\"\n    nsmap: dict[str, str] = {}\n    for m in XMLNS_RE.finditer(xml_text):\n        pfx = m.group(\"pfx\") or \"\"\n        uri = m.group(\"uri\")\n        # ET.register_namespace expects prefix '' for default namespace.\n        if pfx not in nsmap:\n            nsmap[pfx] = uri\n            ET.register_namespace(pfx, uri)\n    return nsmap\n\n\nPLACEHOLDER_RE = re.compile(r\"{{\\s*([^{}]+?)\\s*}}\")\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass TextRun:\n    elem: ET.Element\n    start: int\n    end: int\n\n\ndef _collect_hp_t_elems(p_elem: ET.Element, hp_uri: str) -> list[ET.Element]:\n    t_tag = f\"{{{hp_uri}}}t\"\n    # Walk in document order within paragraph.\n    return [e for e in p_elem.iter() if e.tag == t_tag]\n\n\ndef _concat_text(elems: list[ET.Element]) -> str:\n    return \"\".join([(e.text or \"\") for e in elems])\n\n\ndef _compute_text_ranges(t_elems: list[ET.Element]) -> list[TextRun]:\n    ranges: list[TextRun] = []\n    pos = 0\n    for e in t_elems:\n        s = pos\n        pos += len(e.text or \"\")\n        ranges.append(TextRun(e, s, pos))\n    return ranges\n\n\ndef _placeholder_spans_multiple_nodes(full_text: str, ranges: list[TextRun]) -> bool:\n    for m in PLACEHOLDER_RE.finditer(full_text):\n        ms, me = m.span()\n        containing = [r for r in ranges if r.start <= ms and me <= r.end]\n        if not containing:\n            # Spans across at least two nodes.\n            return True\n    return False\n\n\ndef _merge_t_nodes(p_elem: ET.Element, t_elems: list[ET.Element], hp_uri: str) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Merge all paragraph text into the first <hp:t>, remove the rest.\"\"\"\n    if not t_elems:\n        return\n    full_text = _concat_text(t_elems)\n    t_elems[0].text = full_text\n\n    t_tag = f\"{{{hp_uri}}}t\"\n    # Remove remaining <hp:t> elements. Must remove from their direct parent.\n    for t in t_elems[1:]:\n        parent = _find_parent(p_elem, t)\n        if parent is not None:\n            parent.remove(t)\n\n    # Also remove any now-empty <hp:run> elements (optional but keeps XML tidy).\n    run_tag = f\"{{{hp_uri}}}run\"\n    for run in list(p_elem.iter(run_tag)):\n        if len(list(run)) == 0 and (run.text is None or run.text.strip() == \"\"):\n            parent = _find_parent(p_elem, run)\n            if parent is not None:\n                parent.remove(run)\n\n    # Ensure only one <hp:t> remains with the combined text (common expectation).\n    # If the first <hp:t> was removed due to an unexpected structure, re-add it.\n    # (Should not happen in our templates, but defensive.)\n    if not any(e.tag == t_tag for e in p_elem.iter()):\n        run = ET.SubElement(p_elem, run_tag)\n        t = ET.SubElement(run, t_tag)\n        t.text = full_text\n\n\ndef _find_parent(root: ET.Element, child: ET.Element) -> ET.Element | None:\n    for p in root.iter():\n        for c in list(p):\n            if c is child:\n                return p\n    return None\n\n\ndef _replace_placeholders_in_text(text: str, data: dict) -> tuple[str, bool]:\n    changed = False\n\n    def repl(m: re.Match) -> str:\n        nonlocal changed\n        key = m.group(1)\n        if key in data:\n            val = sanitize_value(data[key])\n            changed = True\n            return str(val)\n        return m.group(0)\n\n    new_text = PLACEHOLDER_RE.sub(repl, text)\n    return new_text, changed\n\n\nDATE_RE = re.compile(r\"(?P<y>\\d{4})[.-](?P<m>\\d{1,2})\")\n\n\ndef _month_span_inclusive(start_y: int, start_m: int, end_y: int, end_m: int) -> int:\n    return (end_y - start_y) * 12 + (end_m - start_m) + 1\n\n\ndef _append_phase_months(text: str) -> tuple[str, bool]:\n    \"\"\"Append ' (N개월)' to phase lines that include a date or date range.\n\n    Supports strings like:\n      '1단계: ... (2026-08 ~ 2026-10)'\n      '2단계: ... (2026.10 ~ 2026.12)'\n      '3단계: ... (2027-01)'\n\n    Only appends if a suffix like '(N개월)' is not already present.\n    \"\"\"\n    if \"개월\" in text:\n        return text, False\n\n    # Must be a phase line.\n    if not (\"1단계\" in text or \"2단계\" in text or \"3단계\" in text or \"단계1\" in text or \"단계2\" in text or \"단계3\" in text):\n        return text, False\n\n    # Extract the area where dates usually live (inside parentheses), else scan whole text.\n    m_paren = re.search(r\"\\(([^)]*)\\)\", text)\n    date_area = m_paren.group(1) if m_paren else text\n\n    dates = [(int(m.group(\"y\")), int(m.group(\"m\"))) for m in DATE_RE.finditer(date_area)]\n    if not dates:\n        return text, False\n\n    if len(dates) >= 2:\n        (sy, sm), (ey, em) = dates[0], dates[1]\n        months = _month_span_inclusive(sy, sm, ey, em)\n    else:\n        months = 1\n\n    return f\"{text} ({months}개월)\", True\n\n\ndef _remove_linesegarray(p_elem: ET.Element, hp_uri: str) -> bool:\n    removed = False\n    ls_tag = f\"{{{hp_uri}}}linesegarray\"\n    for child in list(p_elem):\n        if child.tag == ls_tag:\n            p_elem.remove(child)\n            removed = True\n    return removed\n\n\ndef process_section_xml(xml_path: Path, data: dict) -> None:\n    xml_text = xml_path.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\")\n    nsmap = register_namespaces_from_xml_text(xml_text)\n\n    hp_uri = nsmap.get(\"hp\")\n    if not hp_uri:\n        raise RuntimeError(f\"Missing 'hp' namespace in {xml_path}\")\n\n    tree = ET.ElementTree(ET.fromstring(xml_text))\n    root = tree.getroot()\n\n    p_tag = f\"{{{hp_uri}}}p\"\n\n    placeholders_remaining = []\n\n    for p in root.iter(p_tag):\n        # Collect text nodes.\n        t_elems = _collect_hp_t_elems(p, hp_uri)\n        if not t_elems:\n            continue\n\n        before_text = _concat_text(t_elems)\n\n        # Detect and merge split placeholders.\n        ranges = _compute_text_ranges(t_elems)\n        full_text = before_text\n        if \"{{\" in full_text and \"}}\" in full_text and _placeholder_spans_multiple_nodes(full_text, ranges):\n            _merge_t_nodes(p, t_elems, hp_uri)\n            # Re-collect after merge.\n            t_elems = _collect_hp_t_elems(p, hp_uri)\n\n        text_changed = False\n\n        # Replace placeholders (per node; merged case will have just one node).\n        for t in t_elems:\n            orig = t.text or \"\"\n            new, changed = _replace_placeholders_in_text(orig, data)\n            if changed:\n                t.text = new\n                text_changed = True\n\n        # Phase month suffix: operate on the concatenated paragraph text.\n        # If there are multiple <hp:t>, update the first and keep the rest as-is.\n        t_elems = _collect_hp_t_elems(p, hp_uri)\n        after_repl_text = _concat_text(t_elems)\n        after_phase_text, phase_changed = _append_phase_months(after_repl_text)\n        if phase_changed:\n            #
